### greet-zh

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は信頼性を保証し、UDP は保証しません。TCP は届いたか・順番どおりかを確認して再送するので遅くなりやすく、UDP は確認せず送るだけなので速く軽いのが特徴です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama local con commits que aún no has compartido, y así dejar un historial lineal y limpio. Usa merge cuando la rama ya esté publicada o la usen otras personas, porque reescribir commits compartidos rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、可靠有序，像打电话，保证数据送达且按序；UDP 无连接、不保证可靠，像寄明信片，速度快、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for a branch only you are working on, before it's shared or merged. Merge when the branch is shared or public, or when you want to keep the record of how and when the work came together.
```

  [opened a task] title: Bun 1.4 Linux bun build --compile 的坑（一页文档） | place: {"kind":"browser","profile":"default","device":"local"} | runs on computer: the home (Ducter's home, called "local" in a place) | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："帮我查一下 Bun 1.4 在 Linux 上用 bun build --compile 有哪些坑，整理成一页文档"。语言：用户用简体中文，文档用简体中文写。 ⏎  ⏎ 要做的： ⏎ 1. 查 Bun 1.4 版本的官方文档、发行说明（release notes）、GitHub issues（oven-sh/bun）里关于 bun build --compile 在 Linux 上的问题与坑。重点覆盖：glibc 与 musl（Alpine）兼容性、--target 选项（linux-x64、linux-arm64、baseline、modern 等）、动态链接与系统库依赖、ldd 结果、体积与启动耗时、--compile 时资源/静态文件/assets 的嵌入与读取路径、process.execPath 与 import.meta 路径、node:fs 读取嵌入文件的限制、native 模块（.node 插件、sqlite、sharp 等）打包问题、环境变量与 bunfig 的影响、CPU 指令集（AVX2 等）导致的非法指令、权限与 chmod、Docker 镜像中运行的问题。 ⏎ 2. 每条坑给出：现象、触发条件、规避办法，并注明来源链接（官方文档或 issue 编号）。 ⏎ 3. 只列在 Bun 1.4 上仍然相关的；若某坑已在 1.4 修复，注明修复版本；查不到依据的不要写。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准：一页以内的 Markdown 文档，按"坑 → 现象 → 规避"组织，末尾附来源链接列表。报告时给出文档正文和你实际访问过的来源。若某些条目无法确认，明确标注"未证实"。 ⏎  ⏎ 不在范围内：不要修改任何代码或仓库，不要提交、发布、发帖、登录或付款。只做查阅与整理。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：浏览器（公开网页查阅即可，不需要登录）。

```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data, so after a crash it can replay the log and recover any committed work. Because writes are sequential appends, it also keeps commits fast.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我让那个 compaction 的任务把 review 里说的锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把锁的范围缩小（只锁真正需要保护的部分），改动在这个任务原来的分支上提交。

完成标准：
- 改动已提交到原分支；
- 相关测试跑过，把结果（通过/失败的数量）报告出来；
- 说明改了哪些地方的锁、各自缩小成了什么范围。

不要做：不合并到主分支，不推送，不改 PR 的状态（仍保持 draft）。推送和合并等用户点头再做。
```

    var val=document.createElement('div');val.className='k-row-value';val.textContent=t.price+' 元';
    row.appendChild(left);row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if(typeof ducter.summary==='function'){ducter.summary(cheap.length+' 班 600 元以下');}
}
function load(){Promise.resolve(ducter.get('fares')).then(show);}
load();
ducter.onChange(load);

      row.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click', function () {
        var target = list[i];
        var all = allFeeds().filter(function (x) { return x !== target && !(x.d === target.d && x.t === target.t && x.ml === target.ml); });
        ducter.set('feeds', all);
      });
      box.appendChild(row);
    });

    ducter.summary(list.length ? ('今天 ' + list.length + ' 次,合计 ' + total + 'ml') : '还没记');
  }

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim() || nowT;
    var ml = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var all = allFeeds();
    all.push({ d: today, t: t.length === 4 ? '0' + t : t, ml: ml });
    ducter.set('feeds', all);
    document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
    document.getElementById('t').value = '';
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
  });

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land

  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2249 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting on someone</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">3</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="waiting"></div>
<h2 class="k-h">Draft</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="drafts"></div>
</main>
<style>
.k-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px}
.k-chip{min-width:36px;height:36px;border-radius:50%;border:1px solid var(--d-line);background:var(--d-card);color:var(--d-ink);font-size:16px;cursor:pointer}
.k-chip[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--d-good);color:var(--d-card);border-color:var(--d-good)}
.k-landed .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-row-main{flex:1;min-width:0}
</style>
<script>
var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
var landed = {};
function row(p){
  var on = !!landed[p.id];
  return '<div class="k-row' + (on ? ' k-landed' : '') + '">' +
    '<button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="' + on + '" data-id="' + p.id + '" aria-label="Landed">' + (on ? '✓' : '') + '</button>' +
    '<div class="k-row-main"><div>' + p.what + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + p.repo + ' · ' + p.wait + '</div></div>' +
    '</div>';
}
function paint(){
  var w = PRS.filter(function(p){return p.group === 'w';});
  var d = PRS.filter(function(p){return p.group === 'd';});
  document.getElementById('waiting').innerHTML = w.map(row).join('');
  document.getElementById('drafts').innerHTML = d.map(row).join('');
  var open = w.filter(function(p){return !landed[p.id];}).length;
  document.getElementById('open').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open ? 'Tick each one off as it lands' : 'All of them have landed';
  if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(open + ' open, ' + (w.length - open) + ' landed');
}
function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){ landed = v || {}; paint(); }).catch(paint);
}
document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
  var b = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
  if (!b) return;
  var id = b.getAttribute('data-id');
  landed[id] = !landed[id];
  ducter.set('landed', landed);
  paint();
});
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter412","group":"w","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","wait":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem10287","group":"w","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","wait":"CI failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","group":"w","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","wait":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter418","group":"d","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","wait":"Still a draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><p class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记一笔。</p></div>
<details class="k-add" id="form"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>说明</label><input class="k-input" id="note"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
<button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通", editing=-1;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
function items(){return ducter.get("items")||[]}
function draw(){
  const xs=items();
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(+x.amt||0),0);
 … mary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN"):"还没记");
}
function edit(i){
  const x=items()[i]; editing=i;
  $("trip").value=x.trip||""; $("day").value=x.day||""; $("note").value=x.note||""; $("amt").value=x.amt;
  setCat(x.cat||"交通"); $("save").textContent="保存修改"; $("del").hidden=false; $("form").open=true;
}
function reset(){
  editing=-1; $("trip").value=""; $("note").value=""; $("amt").value=""; $("save").textContent="保存"; $("del").hidden=true;
}
function setCat(c){cat=c; document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",b.dataset.c===c))}
document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setCat(b.dataset.c));
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(isNaN(amt)) return;
  const xs=items();
  const rec={trip:$("trip").value,day:$("day").value,cat:cat,note:$("note").value,amt:amt};
  if(editing>=0) xs[editing]=rec; else xs.push(rec);
  ducter.set("items",xs); reset(); $("form").open=false; draw();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{
  if(editing<0) return;
  const xs=items(); xs.splice(editing,1);
  ducter.set("items",xs); reset(); $("form").open=false; draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","note":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amt":1036}]}

(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 我去给 TTL 测试补一条正好到期那一刻的用例，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

请在现有的 TTL 假时钟测试基础上，再加一条测试，覆盖时间恰好等于过期时刻的情况：
- 设置 TTL 为 1 秒，推进假时钟到恰好 1 秒（不多不少），断言此时值已过期（或按现有约定的边界语义断言，并在报告里说明你定的边界是"到期即失效"还是"到期后才失效"）。
- 再推进 1 毫秒前后各验证一次：到期前一刻仍有值，到期那一刻按约定处理。
- 只改测试相关代码，不改 Cache 的 TTL 实现，除非发现边界确实有 bug（有的话先在报告里说明，不要擅自改语义）。
- 完成后运行整个测试文件，报告通过数量，并确认新测试在假时钟下稳定（连跑 20 次都过）。

范围外：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。在该任务的分支上提交即可。
```
